hero · Round 1 · prepared

Render only brand 'Narrowtide' and product label 'CARRY VEST', exactly spelled, in large restrained lettering. No other text, no real brands, no human faces. This is a simple everyday physical product, not a building or equipment installation. 3:4 portrait studio composition, high-end packshot materials and controlled grounded shadows.
